- **ops-rework-loop**: Label-triggered loop that acts on PR review feedback. When a reviewer has left comments and labels a PR `ops/auto-rework`, it reads every actionable point — and any CI failure the PR's own change caused — commands `ops-change · implement` scoped to that and `ops-change · verify`, pushes, replies on the threads, re-requests review, and clears the label — then stops.
